\displaystyle\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{+},j\not\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{+}}l_{1,(i,j)}\sum_{p\in[P]}[\theta_{i,j}^{(p)}(\mathbf{v})]^{2}=\frac{(1-\lambda)^{2}}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{+},j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{1}^{+}\cup{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{-}\cup{\mathcal{S}}_{1}^{-}}l_{1,(i,j)}\pm o\bigg(\frac{1}{\mathrm{polylog}(n)}\bigg)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{+},j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{+}}l_{1,(i,j)}\sum_{p\in[P]}[\theta_{i,j}^{(p)}(\mathbf{v})]^{2}=\frac{\ell_{1,(i,i)}}{n^{2}}+\frac{1+[(1-\lambda)^{2}+\lambda^{2}](P-1)}{Pn^{2}}\cdot\sum_{i\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{+},j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{+},j\neq i}l_{1,(i,j)}\pm o\bigg(\frac{1}{\mathrm{polylog}(n)}\bigg).

Similarly, we can also obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\not\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{+},j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{+}}l_{1,(i,j)}\sum_{p\in[P]}[\theta_{i,j}^{(p)}=\frac{(1-\lambda)^{2}}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\in{\mathcal{S}}_{1}^{+}\cup{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{-}\cup{\mathcal{S}}_{1}^{-},j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{+}}l_{1,(i,j)}\pm o\big(1/\mathrm{polylog}(n)\big)

Therefore, combining the above results, we can get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{+}\text{ or }j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{+}}l_{1,(i,j)}\sum_{p\in[P]}[\theta_{i,j}^{(p)}(\mathbf{v})]^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{+},j\not\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{+}}l_{1,(i,j)}\sum_{p\in[P]}[\theta_{i,j}^{(p)}(\mathbf{v})]^{2}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{+},j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{+}}l_{1,(i,j)}\sum_{p\in[P]}[\theta_{i,j}^{(p)}(\mathbf{v})]^{2}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\not\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{+},j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{+}}l_{1,(i,j)}\sum_{p\in[P]}[\theta_{i,j}^{(p)}(\mathbf{v})]^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{(1-\lambda)^{2}}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{+},j\not\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{+}\text{ or }i\not\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{+},j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{+}}l_{1,(i,j)}+\frac{\ell_{1,(i,i)}}{n^{2}}+\frac{1+[(1-\lambda)^{2}+\lambda^{2}](P-1)}{Pn^{2}}\cdot\sum_{i\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{+},j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{+},j\neq i}l_{1,(i,j)}

\displaystyle\qquad\pm o\bigg(\frac{1}{\mathrm{polylog}(n)}\bigg).

Similarly, we can get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{-}\text{ or }j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{-}}l_{1,(i,j)}\sum_{p\in[P]}[\theta_{i,j}^{(p)}(\mathbf{u})]^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{-},j\not\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{-}}l_{1,(i,j)}\sum_{p\in[P]}[\theta_{i,j}^{(p)}(\mathbf{u})]^{2}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{-},j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{-}}l_{1,(i,j)}\sum_{p\in[P]}[\theta_{i,j}^{(p)}(\mathbf{u})]^{2}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\not\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{-},j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{-}}l_{1,(i,j)}\sum_{p\in[P]}[\theta_{i,j}^{(p)}(\mathbf{u})]^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{(1-\lambda)^{2}}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{-},j\not\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}-\text{ or }i\not\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{-},j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{-}}l_{1,(i,j)}+\frac{\ell_{1,(i,i)}}{n^{2}}+\frac{1+[(1-\lambda)^{2}+\lambda^{2}](P-1)}{Pn^{2}}\cdot\sum_{i\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{-},j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{-},j\neq i}l_{1,(i,j)}

\displaystyle\qquad\pm o\bigg(\frac{1}{\mathrm{polylog}(n)}\bigg).

Then note that the positive and negative data are generated with equal probability, we have
|{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{-}|
and
|{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{+}|
are different by at most
o\big(1/\mathrm{polylog}(n)\big)
, therefore, it is easy to get that

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{+}\text{ or }j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{+}}l_{1,(i,j)}\sum_{p\in[P]}[\theta_{i,j}^{(p)}(\mathbf{v})]^{2}-\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{-}\text{ or }j\in{\mathcal{S}}_{0}^{-}}l_{2,(i,j)}\sum_{p\in[P]}[\theta_{i,j}^{(p)}(\mathbf{u})]^{2}\bigg|\leq o\big(1/\mathrm{polylog}(n)\big).

Plugging the above inequality into (
B.4
) we can conclude that

\displaystyle|\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{v})-\gamma_{2}^{(t)}(\mathbf{u},\mathbf{u})|\leq o\bigg(\frac{1}{\mathrm{polylog}(n)}\bigg).

This completes the proof.
∎

Finally, we state the outcome of noise learning, common feature learning, and rare feature learning in the following Lemma.

Lemma B.15
.

Let
\zeta
be a preset quantity satisfying
\zeta=[\omega(d\sigma_{p}^{2}/(Pn)),o(d^{-1/2}\sigma_{p}^{-1})]
and
T
be the smallest iteration number such that
\max_{k\in[2],(i,j)\in{\mathcal{S}}}|F_{k}(\mathbf{W}^{(T)};\mathbf{x}_{i,j})|\geq\zeta/2
, then with probability at least
1-1/\mathrm{poly}(n)
, it holds that

\displaystyle\max_{r}|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(T)},\mathbf{v}\rangle|,\max_{r}|\langle\mathbf{w}_{2,r}^{(T)},\mathbf{u}\rangle|=\Omega\bigg(\frac{\zeta^{1/2}}{m^{1/2}}\bigg),\quad\max_{r}|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(T)},\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\rangle|,\max_{r}|\langle\mathbf{w}_{2,r}^{(T)},\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\rangle|=\Omega\bigg(\frac{\rho\zeta^{1/2}}{Pm^{1/2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\max_{r}|\langle\mathbf{w}_{2,r}^{(T)},\mathbf{v}\rangle|,\max_{r}|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(T)},\mathbf{u}\rangle|=\widetilde{O}(\zeta^{3/2}),\quad\max_{r}|\langle\mathbf{w}_{2,r}^{(T)},\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\rangle|,\max_{r}|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(T)},\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\rangle|=\widetilde{O}(\zeta^{3/2})

Proof.

We will only prove the results for the inner products
\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle
,

\langle\mathbf{w}_{2,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}\rangle
,

\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\rangle
,
\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}\rangle
, and
\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\rangle
, as the proof for the remaining inner products will be exactly the same.

We first recall the update of
\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r},\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\rangle
:

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t+1)},\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\rangle

\displaystyle=\big[1+\eta\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{v}^{\prime},\mathbf{v}^{\prime})\big]\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\rangle+\eta\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{v}^{\prime})\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle+\eta\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{u},\mathbf{v}^{\prime})\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad+\eta\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{u}^{\prime},\mathbf{v}^{\prime})\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\rangle+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{p\in[P]}\eta\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\bm{\xi}_{i}^{(p)},\mathbf{v}^{\prime})\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\bm{\xi}_{i}^{(p)}\rangle.

The using Lemma
B.7
and the similar proof of Lemma
B.13
, we can get

\displaystyle\big|\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{u},\mathbf{v}^{\prime})\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}\rangle\big|=O\bigg(\frac{\zeta\rho}{P}\bigg)\cdot|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}\rangle|=O\bigg(\frac{\zeta\rho\log^{2}(n)}{P}\bigg)\cdot|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle|

\displaystyle\big|\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{u}^{\prime},\mathbf{v}^{\prime})\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}\rangle\big|=O\bigg(\frac{\zeta\rho^{2}}{P}\bigg)\cdot|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}\rangle|=O\bigg(\frac{\zeta\rho^{2}\log^{2}(n)}{P}\bigg)\cdot|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle|

\displaystyle\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{p\in[P]}\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\bm{\xi}_{i}^{(p)},\mathbf{v}^{\prime})\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\bm{\xi}_{i}^{(p)}\rangle\bigg|\leq O\bigg(\bigg(\frac{\rho d^{1/2}\sigma_{p}}{P^{1/2}n^{1/2}}+\rho\zeta d^{1/2}\sigma_{p}\bigg)\cdot|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle|\bigg).

Therefore, noting that we have assumed
d\sigma_{p}=o(n/P)
and
\zeta=o\big(\frac{1}{Pd^{1/2}\sigma_{p}}\big)
,

\displaystyle\big|\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{u},\mathbf{v}^{\prime})\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}\rangle\big|,\big|\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{u}^{\prime},\mathbf{v}^{\prime})\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\rangle\big|,\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{p\in[P]}\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\bm{\xi}_{i}^{(p)},\mathbf{v}^{\prime})\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\bm{\xi}_{i}^{(p)}\rangle\bigg|\leq c\cdot|\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{v}^{\prime})\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle|

for some sufficiently small constant
c<0.5
.
Therefore, further applying Lemma
B.7
, we can get that

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t+1)},\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\rangle

\displaystyle=\big[1+\Theta(\eta\rho)\big]\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\rangle+\Theta(\eta\rho/P)\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle

(B.25)

Given the above equation, we are able to complete the proof by combining it with Lemma
B.13
:

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t+1)},\mathbf{v}\rangle=\big[1+\Theta(\eta)\big]\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle.

(B.26)

In particular, given the fact that
|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(0)},\mathbf{v}\rangle|=\Omega(\sigma_{0})
, we can get the following

\displaystyle|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(T)},\mathbf{v}\rangle|=\Omega\bigg(\frac{\zeta^{1/2}}{m^{1/2}}\bigg)

(B.27)

for some
T=O\big(\frac{\log(\zeta/(m\sigma_{0}))}{\eta}\big)
. Besides, by Lemma
B.14
and (
B.15
), we have for any
r^{\prime}\in[m]
,

\displaystyle\frac{|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t+1)},\mathbf{u}\rangle|}{|\langle\mathbf{w}_{2,r^{\prime}}^{(t+1)},\mathbf{u}\rangle|}

\displaystyle=\frac{|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle|}{|\langle\mathbf{w}_{2,r^{\prime}}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}\rangle|}\cdot\bigg(\frac{1+\eta\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{v})\pm o\big(\eta/\mathrm{polylog}(n)\big)}{1+\eta\gamma_{2}^{(t)}(\mathbf{u},\mathbf{u})\pm o\big(\eta/\mathrm{polylog}(n)\big)}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle|}{|\langle\mathbf{w}_{2,r^{\prime}}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}\rangle|}\cdot\big[1+\eta\cdot\big(\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{v})-\gamma_{2}^{(t)}(\mathbf{u},\mathbf{u})\big)\pm o\big(\eta/\mathrm{polylog}(n)\big)\big]

\displaystyle=\frac{|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(0)},\mathbf{v}\rangle|}{|\langle\mathbf{w}_{2,r^{\prime}}^{(0)},\mathbf{u}\rangle|}\cdot\big[1\pm o\big(\eta/\mathrm{polylog}(n)\big)\big]^{t}.

Note that
t\leq T=\widetilde{O}(1/\eta)
, we can further get
\frac{|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t+1)},\mathbf{u}\rangle|}{|\langle\mathbf{w}_{2,r^{\prime}}^{(t+1)},\mathbf{u}\rangle|}=\Theta(1)\cdot\frac{|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(0)},\mathbf{v}\rangle|}{|\langle\mathbf{w}_{2,r^{\prime}}^{(0)},\mathbf{u}\rangle|}
. This immediately implies that
\max_{r}|\langle\mathbf{w}_{2,r}^{(T)},\mathbf{u}\rangle|=\Theta(\max_{r}|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(T)},\mathbf{v}\rangle|)=\Omega\big(\zeta^{1/2}/m^{1/2}\big)
.

Moreover, (
B.25
) implies that

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(T)},\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\rangle

\displaystyle=\big[1+\Theta(\eta\rho)\big]^{T}\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(0)},\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\rangle+\Theta(\eta\rho/P)\cdot\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\big[1+\Theta(\eta\rho)\big]^{t}\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle.

Further note that
\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle
has the same sign for all
t\leq T
and
[1+\Theta(\eta\rho)]^{t}=\Theta(1)
for all
t\leq T
, then define
T^{\prime}=T-\Theta(1/\eta)
, we have

\displaystyle|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(T)},\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\rangle|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\Theta(1)\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(0)},\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\rangle+\Theta(\eta\rho/P)\cdot\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle\geq\Theta(\eta\rho/P)\cdot\bigg|\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle\bigg|-\Theta(1)\cdot\big|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(0)},\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\geq\Theta(\eta\rho/P)\cdot\sum_{t=T^{\prime}}^{T-1}\big|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle\big|-\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0}).

Then by (
B.26
) and (
B.27
), we have for all
t\in[T^{\prime},T-1]
, it holds that

\displaystyle|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle|=\Theta\big(|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(T)},\mathbf{v}\rangle|\big)=\Omega\bigg(\frac{\zeta^{1/2}}{m^{1/2}}\bigg).

Therefore, we can finally get

\displaystyle|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(T)},\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\rangle|

\displaystyle\geq\Theta\bigg(\frac{(T-T^{\prime})\eta\rho}{P}\cdot\bigg)\cdot\Omega\bigg(\frac{\zeta^{1/2}}{m^{1/2}}\bigg)-\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})

\displaystyle=\Omega\bigg(\frac{\rho\zeta^{1/2}}{Pm^{1/2}}\bigg).

The remaining part is to establish the upper bounds in terms of incorrect feature learning, i.e.,
\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(T)},\mathbf{u}\rangle
and
\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(T)},\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\rangle
. Particularly, recall their update forms as follows:

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t+1)},\mathbf{u}\rangle

\displaystyle=\big[1-\eta\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{u},\mathbf{u})\big]\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}\rangle+\eta\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{u})\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle+\eta\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{v}^{\prime},\mathbf{u})\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad+\eta\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{u}^{\prime},\mathbf{u}^{\prime})\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\rangle+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{p\in[P]}\eta\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\bm{\xi}_{i}^{(p)},\mathbf{u})\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\bm{\xi}_{i}^{(p)}\rangle,

\displaystyle\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t+1)},\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\rangle

\displaystyle=\big[1-\eta\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{u}^{\prime},\mathbf{u}^{\prime})\big]\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\rangle+\eta\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{v},\mathbf{u}^{\prime})\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle+\eta\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{u},\mathbf{u}^{\prime})\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad+\eta\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{v}^{\prime},\mathbf{u}^{\prime})\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\rangle+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{p\in[P]}\eta\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\bm{\xi}_{i}^{(p)},\mathbf{u}^{\prime})\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\bm{\xi}_{i}^{(p)}\rangle.

Then by Lemmas
B.6
and
B.10
, we have

\displaystyle\max\big\{|\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{u}^{\prime},\mathbf{u})|,\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{u},\mathbf{u}^{\prime})|\big\}\leq\min\big\{\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{u},\mathbf{u}),\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\mathbf{u}^{\prime},\mathbf{u}^{\prime})\big\},

the above equations further yield

\displaystyle|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t+1)},\mathbf{u}\rangle|+|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t+1)},\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}\rangle|+|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\rangle|+O(\eta\zeta)\cdot|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle|+O(\eta\zeta\rho/P)\cdot|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\rangle|

\displaystyle\qquad+\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{p\in[P]}\eta\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\bm{\xi}_{i}^{(p)},\mathbf{u})\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\bm{\xi}_{i}^{(p)}\rangle\bigg|+\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{p\in[P]}\eta\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\bm{\xi}_{i}^{(p)},\mathbf{u}^{\prime})\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\bm{\xi}_{i}^{(p)}\rangle\bigg|.

Then using the fact that
T\eta=O(\mathrm{polylog}(n))
, we can further obtain

\displaystyle|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(T)},\mathbf{u}\rangle|+|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(T)},\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\rangle|\leq\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})+\widetilde{O}(\zeta)\cdot\max_{t\in[T]}|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle|+\widetilde{O}(\zeta\rho/P)\cdot\max_{t\in[T]}|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\rangle|

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\bigg[\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{p\in[P]}\eta\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\bm{\xi}_{i}^{(p)},\mathbf{u})\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\bm{\xi}_{i}^{(p)}\rangle\bigg|+\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{p\in[P]}\eta\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\bm{\xi}_{i}^{(p)},\mathbf{u}^{\prime})\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\bm{\xi}_{i}^{(p)}\rangle\bigg|\bigg].

Moreover, following the same procedure of (
B.4
), we can get

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{p\in[P]}\eta\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\bm{\xi}_{i}^{(p)},\mathbf{u})\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\bm{\xi}_{i}^{(p)}\rangle\bigg|,\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{p\in[P]}\eta\gamma_{1}^{(t)}(\bm{\xi}_{i}^{(p)},\mathbf{u}^{\prime})\cdot\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\bm{\xi}_{i}^{(p)}\rangle\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})+\widetilde{O}\bigg(\frac{d\sigma_{p}^{2}}{Pn}\bigg)\cdot\max_{t\in[T]}\big[|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle|+|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}\rangle|+|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\rangle|+|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\rangle|\big]

Finally, using the assumption that
\zeta=\omega(d\sigma_{p}^{2}/(Pn))
, we can get that

\displaystyle|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(T)},\mathbf{u}\rangle|+|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(T)},\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})+\widetilde{O}(\zeta)\cdot\Big[\max_{t\in[T]}|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle|+\max_{t\in[T]}|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\rangle|+\max_{t\in[T]}\big[|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}\rangle|+|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\rangle|\big]\Big].

Besides, note that the above inequality actually holds for any
T^{\prime}\leq T
, thus

\displaystyle|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(T^{\prime})},\mathbf{u}\rangle|+|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(T^{\prime})},\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})+\widetilde{O}(\zeta)\cdot\Big[\max_{t\in[T^{\prime}]}|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle|+\max_{t\in[T^{\prime}]}|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\rangle|+\max_{t\in[T^{\prime}]}\big[|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}\rangle|+|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\rangle|\big]\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})+\widetilde{O}(\zeta)\cdot\Big[\max_{t\in[T]}|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle|+\max_{t\in[T]}|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\rangle|+\max_{t\in[T]}\big[|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}\rangle|+|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\rangle|\big]\Big].

This further implies that

\displaystyle\max_{t\in[T]}\big[|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}\rangle|+|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\rangle|\big]

\displaystyle\leq\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})+\widetilde{O}(\zeta)\cdot\Big[\max_{t\in[T]}|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle|+\max_{t\in[T]}|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\rangle|+\max_{t\in[T]}\big[|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}\rangle|+|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\rangle|\big]\Big].

Then, rearranging terms will readily give the following result:

\displaystyle|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(T)},\mathbf{u}\rangle|+|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(T)},\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{t\in[T]}\big[|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}\rangle|+|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\rangle|\big]

\displaystyle\leq\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{0})+\widetilde{O}(\zeta)\cdot\big[\max_{t\in[T]}\big[|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle|+\max_{t\in[T]}\big[|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\rangle|\big]

\displaystyle\leq\widetilde{O}(\zeta^{3/2}),

where the last inequality holds since we must have

\displaystyle\max_{t\in[T]}|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle|,\max_{t\in[T]}|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\rangle|=O\big(\log(n)\cdot\zeta^{1/2}\big)

as otherwise, we cannot have

\max_{k\in[2],(i,j)\in{\mathcal{S}}}|F_{k}(\mathbf{W}^{(T)};\mathbf{x}_{i,j})|\leq\zeta/2
for all
t\leq T
, which contradicts the condition made in this lemma. This completes the upper bounds of
|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(T)},\mathbf{u}\rangle|
and
|\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(T)},\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\rangle|
.

∎

B.5
Proof of Theorem
4.2

Proof of Theorem
4.2
.

We will evaluate the test error for common feature data and rare feature data separately. In particular, take the positive data
(\mathbf{x},1)
as an example. Then note that the data
\mathbf{x}
consists of the common feature
\mathbf{v}
, we can obtain the following by Lemma
5.6
:

\displaystyle F_{1}(\mathbf{W}^{(t)};\mathbf{x})=\sum_{r=1}^{m}\sum_{p=1}^{P}\big(\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}^{(p)}\rangle\big)^{2}\geq\sum_{r=1}^{m}\sum_{p:\mathbf{x}^{(p)}=\mathbf{v}}\big(\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle\big)^{2}=\widetilde{\Omega}(\zeta).

On the other hand, we can follow the similar proof of Theorem
4.1
to show that
|\langle\mathbf{w}_{k,r}^{(T)},\bm{\zeta}\rangle|^{2}=\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{p}^{2}n^{2})
with probability at least
1-1/\mathrm{poly}(n)
, then it follows that

\displaystyle F_{2}(\mathbf{W}^{(t)};\mathbf{x})=\sum_{r=1}^{m}\sum_{p=1}^{P}\big(\langle\mathbf{w}_{2,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}^{(p)}\rangle\big)^{2}\leq\widetilde{O}(b\alpha^{2}\zeta^{3})+\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{p}^{2}n^{2})<F_{1}(\mathbf{W}^{(t)};\mathbf{x}).

where we use the fact that
b\alpha^{2}=o(1/\mathrm{polylog}(n))
and
d=\omega(n^{3}P)
.
This clearly suggests that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{(\mathbf{x},y)\sim\mathcal{D}_{\mathrm{common}}}[\mathop{\mathrm{argmax}}_{k}F_{k}(\mathbf{W}^{(t)},\mathbf{x})\neq y]\leq\frac{1}{\mathrm{poly}(n)}.

Then let’s move on to the rare feature data. In particular, consider the positive rare feature data
(\mathbf{x},1)
, which contains the rare feature
\mathbf{v}^{\prime}
, we have

\displaystyle F_{1}(\mathbf{W}^{(t)};\mathbf{x})=\sum_{r=1}^{m}\sum_{p=1}^{P}\big(\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}^{(p)}\rangle\big)^{2}\geq\geq\sum_{r=1}^{m}\sum_{p:\mathbf{x}^{(p)}=\mathbf{v}^{\prime}}\big(\langle\mathbf{w}_{1,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}\rangle\big)^{2}=\widetilde{\Omega}(\rho^{2}\zeta).

On the other hand, it holds that

\displaystyle F_{2}(\mathbf{W}^{(t)};\mathbf{x})=\sum_{r=1}^{m}\sum_{p=1}^{P}\big(\langle\mathbf{w}_{2,r}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}^{(p)}\rangle\big)^{2}\leq\widetilde{O}(b\alpha^{2}\zeta^{3})+\widetilde{O}(\sigma_{p}^{2}n^{2})=o(\rho^{2}\zeta)<F_{1}(\mathbf{W}^{(t)};\mathbf{x}),

where we use the fact that
b\alpha^{2}\zeta^{2}=o(\rho)
and
d=\omega(n^{3}P^{3}/\rho^{2})
. Therefore, this implies that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{(\mathbf{x},y)\sim\mathcal{D}_{\mathrm{rare}}}[\mathop{\mathrm{argmax}}_{k}F_{k}(\mathbf{W}^{(t)},\mathbf{x})\neq y]\leq\frac{1}{\mathrm{poly}(n)}.

Putting the results for common feature data and rare feature data together, we are able to complete the proof.

∎
